-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    ~GpuWorkspace() {
+        cudaFree(d_cardinalities_); cudaFree(d_members_); cudaFree(d_states_);
+        cudaFree(d_seeds_); cudaFree(d_distances_); cudaFree(d_clustered_); cudaFree(d_points_);
+    }
+
+    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,
+                            const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered, double threshold,
+                            int& best_cardinality, int& best_seed,
+                            std::vector<int>& best_members) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+        best_cardinality = -1;
+        best_seed = INT_MAX;
+        best_members.clear();
+        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(batch_capacity_, seeds.size() - begin));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, seeds.data() + begin, static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+            constructCandidateClusters<<<count, CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+                d_seeds_, count, d_clustered_, d_distances_, threshold, n_,
+                d_states_, d_members_, d_cardinalities_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_cardinalities_.data(), d_cardinalities_,
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = seeds[begin + i];
+                const int cardinality = host_cardinalities_[i];
+                if (cardinality > best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == best_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {
+                    best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    best_seed = seed;
+                    best_members.resize(cardinality);
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(),
+                                          d_members_ + static_cast<size_t>(i) * n_,
+                                          static_cast<size_t>(cardinality) * sizeof(int),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+                }
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
-            }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+    }
+
+private:
+    MPI_Comm comm_;
+    int n_, batch_capacity_;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    double* d_distances_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_states_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<int> host_cardinalities_;
+};
+
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
+
+    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
+    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+
+    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
+        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
+            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
+
+        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
+        std::vector<int> local_members;
+        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
+                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
+
+        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
+        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
+
+        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
+        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
+        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
+        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
+        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
+            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
+
+        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
+        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
+        #pragma omp parallel
+        {
+            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
+            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
+            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
+            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
+                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
         }
+        unclustered.clear();
+        for (auto& chunk : kept)
+            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate < n && member < n) {
+        const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+        const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+        // Member-major storage makes all candidate loads in a warp contiguous.
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * n + candidate] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
+                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
+    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
+// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           double threshold, int n,
+                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
+                                           int* __restrict__ members,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
+    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        state[seed] = 1;
+        cluster_members[0] = seed;
     }
-

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (member_count < n) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (state[candidate]) continue;
+            double diameter = 0.0;
+            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
+                diameter = fmax(diameter,
+                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
+            }
+            if (diameter < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
+                thread_best_distance = diameter;
+                thread_best_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
+        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
+                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
+                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
+            state[selected] = 1;
+            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (tid == 0) cardinalities[cluster_index] = member_count;

+class GpuWorkspace {
+public:
+    GpuWorkspace(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds, MPI_Comm comm)
+        : comm_(comm), n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        int world_rank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm_, &world_rank);
+        MPI_Comm local_comm;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(comm_, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, world_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);
+        int local_rank = 0, device_count = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), comm_);
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            if (world_rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA-capable GPU required.\n");
+            MPI_Abort(comm_, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % device_count), comm_);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+
+        size_t matrix_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * n_ * sizeof(double);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distances_, matrix_bytes), comm_);
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks((n_ + threads.x - 1) / threads.x,
+                          (n_ + threads.y - 1) / threads.y);

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), comm_);
+

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), comm_);
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char));
+        const size_t affordable = bytes_per_seed == 0 ? 1 : (free_bytes * 3 / 4) / bytes_per_seed;
+        batch_capacity_ = std::max(1, std::min(maximum_local_seeds,
+                                               static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(affordable, INT_MAX))));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_states_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_), comm_);

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        host_cardinalities_.resize(batch_capacity_);

+    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,

+        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {

+private:
+    MPI_Comm comm_;
+    int n_, batch_capacity_;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    double* d_distances_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
+
+    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
+    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+
+    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
+        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
+            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
+
+        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
+        std::vector<int> local_members;
+        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
+                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
+
+        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
+        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
+
+        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
+        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
+        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
+        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
+        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
+            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
+
+        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
+        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
+        #pragma omp parallel
+        {
+            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
+            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
+            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
+            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
+                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
         }
+        unclustered.clear();
+        for (auto& chunk : kept)
+            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate < n && member < n) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
+                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
+    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
+// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           double threshold, int n,
+                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
+                                           int* __restrict__ members,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
+    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        state[seed] = 1;
+        cluster_members[0] = seed;
     }

+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (member_count < n) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (state[candidate]) continue;
+            double diameter = 0.0;
+            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
+                diameter = fmax(diameter,
+                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
+            }
+            if (diameter < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
+                thread_best_distance = diameter;
+                thread_best_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
+        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
+                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
+                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
+            state[selected] = 1;
+            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }

-std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,

+class GpuWorkspace {
+public:
+    GpuWorkspace(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds, MPI_Comm comm)
+        : comm_(comm), n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        int world_rank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm_, &world_rank);
+        MPI_Comm local_comm;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(comm_, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, world_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);
+        int local_rank = 0, device_count = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), comm_);
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            if (world_rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA-capable GPU required.\n");
+            MPI_Abort(comm_, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % device_count), comm_);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+
+        size_t matrix_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * n_ * sizeof(double);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distances_, matrix_bytes), comm_);
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks((n_ + threads.x - 1) / threads.x,
+                          (n_ + threads.y - 1) / threads.y);
+        buildDistanceMatrix<<<blocks, threads>>>(d_points_, d_distances_, n_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), comm_);
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), comm_);
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char));
+        const size_t affordable = bytes_per_seed == 0 ? 1 : (free_bytes * 3 / 4) / bytes_per_seed;
+        batch_capacity_ = std::max(1, std::min(maximum_local_seeds,
+                                               static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(affordable, INT_MAX))));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_states_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_ * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        host_cardinalities_.resize(batch_capacity_);
     }

-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {

+    ~GpuWorkspace() {
+        cudaFree(d_cardinalities_); cudaFree(d_members_); cudaFree(d_states_);
+        cudaFree(d_seeds_); cudaFree(d_distances_); cudaFree(d_clustered_); cudaFree(d_points_);
+    }
+
+    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,
+                            const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered, double threshold,
+                            int& best_cardinality, int& best_seed,
+                            std::vector<int>& best_members) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+        best_cardinality = -1;
+        best_seed = INT_MAX;
+        best_members.clear();
+        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(batch_capacity_, seeds.size() - begin));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, seeds.data() + begin, static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+            constructCandidateClusters<<<count, CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+                d_seeds_, count, d_clustered_, d_distances_, threshold, n_,
+                d_states_, d_members_, d_cardinalities_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_cardinalities_.data(), d_cardinalities_,
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = seeds[begin + i];
+                const int cardinality = host_cardinalities_[i];
+                if (cardinality > best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == best_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {
+                    best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    best_seed = seed;
+                    best_members.resize(cardinality);
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(),
+                                          d_members_ + static_cast<size_t>(i) * n_,
+                                          static_cast<size_t>(cardinality) * sizeof(int),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+                }
             }
         }

-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
-            }

+
+private:
+    MPI_Comm comm_;
+    int n_, batch_capacity_;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    double* d_distances_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_states_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<int> host_cardinalities_;
+};
+
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
+
+    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
+    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+
+    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
+        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
+            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
+
+        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
+        std::vector<int> local_members;
+        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
+                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
+
+        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
+        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
+
+        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
+        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
+        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
+        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
+        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
+            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
+
+        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
+        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
+        #pragma omp parallel
+        {
+            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
+            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
+            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
+            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
+                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
         }
+        unclustered.clear();
+        for (auto& chunk : kept)
+            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

Single-rank and two-rank runs now produce the exact same membership hashes as the sequential baseline, including the deterministic cluster ordering, and validation passes. I also corrected the CUDA compilation path so OpenMP directives are actually enabled rather than merely linked. I’m doing a final scalability-oriented run and source/build audit now.

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate < n && member < n) {
+        const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+        const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
+                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
+    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
+// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           double threshold, int n,
+                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
+                                           int* __restrict__ members,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
+    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        state[seed] = 1;
+        cluster_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (member_count < n) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (state[candidate]) continue;
+            double diameter = 0.0;
+            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
+                diameter = fmax(diameter,
+                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
+            }
+            if (diameter < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
+                thread_best_distance = diameter;
+                thread_best_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
+        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
+                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
+                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
+            state[selected] = 1;
+            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (tid == 0) cardinalities[cluster_index] = member_count;
 }

-std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,

-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
-    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+class GpuWorkspace {
+public:
+    GpuWorkspace(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds, MPI_Comm comm)
+        : comm_(comm), n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        int world_rank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm_, &world_rank);
+        MPI_Comm local_comm;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(comm_, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, world_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);
+        int local_rank = 0, device_count = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), comm_);
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            if (world_rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA-capable GPU required.\n");
+            MPI_Abort(comm_, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % device_count), comm_);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+
+        size_t matrix_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * n_ * sizeof(double);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distances_, matrix_bytes), comm_);
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks((n_ + threads.x - 1) / threads.x,
+                          (n_ + threads.y - 1) / threads.y);
+        buildDistanceMatrix<<<blocks, threads>>>(d_points_, d_distances_, n_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), comm_);
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), comm_);
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char));
+        const size_t affordable = bytes_per_seed == 0 ? 1 : (free_bytes * 3 / 4) / bytes_per_seed;
+        batch_capacity_ = std::max(1, std::min(maximum_local_seeds,
+                                               static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(affordable, INT_MAX))));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_states_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_ * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        host_cardinalities_.resize(batch_capacity_);
     }
-

-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {

-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;

+    ~GpuWorkspace() {
+        cudaFree(d_cardinalities_); cudaFree(d_members_); cudaFree(d_states_);
+        cudaFree(d_seeds_); cudaFree(d_distances_); cudaFree(d_clustered_); cudaFree(d_points_);
+    }
+
+    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,
+                            const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered, double threshold,
+                            int& best_cardinality, int& best_seed,
+                            std::vector<int>& best_members) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+        best_cardinality = -1;
+        best_seed = INT_MAX;
+        best_members.clear();
+        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(batch_capacity_, seeds.size() - begin));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, seeds.data() + begin, static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+            constructCandidateClusters<<<count, CUDA_THREADS>>>(
+                d_seeds_, count, d_clustered_, d_distances_, threshold, n_,
+                d_states_, d_members_, d_cardinalities_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_cardinalities_.data(), d_cardinalities_,
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = seeds[begin + i];
+                const int cardinality = host_cardinalities_[i];
+                if (cardinality > best_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == best_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {
+                    best_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    best_seed = seed;
+                    best_members.resize(cardinality);
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(),
+                                          d_members_ + static_cast<size_t>(i) * n_,
+                                          static_cast<size_t>(cardinality) * sizeof(int),
+                                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+                }
             }
         }

-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
-            }
-

-            unclustered_indices.erase(

-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;

+
+private:
+    MPI_Comm comm_;
+    int n_, batch_capacity_;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    double* d_distances_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_states_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<int> host_cardinalities_;
+};
+
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
+
+    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
+    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+
+    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
+        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
+            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
+
+        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
+        std::vector<int> local_members;
+        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
+                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
+
+        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
+        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
+
+        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
+        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
+        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
+        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
+        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
+            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
+
+        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
+        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
+        #pragma omp parallel
+        {
+            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
+            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
+            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
+            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
+                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
         }
+        unclustered.clear();
+        for (auto& chunk : kept)
+            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)

set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)

    CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native
    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF)

    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;--extra-device-vectorization;-Xptxas=-O3;-Xcompiler=-fopenmp,-Wall,-Wextra>

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (candidate < n && member < n) {
+        const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+        const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+        // Member-major storage makes all candidate loads in a warp contiguous.
+        distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * n + candidate] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
+                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
+    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
+// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           double threshold, int n,
+                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
+                                           int* __restrict__ members,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
+    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        state[seed] = 1;
+        cluster_members[0] = seed;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (member_count < n) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (state[candidate]) continue;
+            double diameter = 0.0;
+            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
+                diameter = fmax(diameter,
+                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
+            }
+            if (diameter < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
+                thread_best_distance = diameter;
+                thread_best_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
+        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
+                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
+                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
+            state[selected] = 1;
+            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (tid == 0) cardinalities[cluster_index] = member_count;
 }

+class GpuWorkspace {
+public:
+    GpuWorkspace(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds, MPI_Comm comm)
+        : comm_(comm), n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        int world_rank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm_, &world_rank);
+        MPI_Comm local_comm;
+        MPI_Comm_split_type(comm_, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, world_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);
+        int local_rank = 0, device_count = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), comm_);
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            if (world_rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA-capable GPU required.\n");
+            MPI_Abort(comm_, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % device_count), comm_);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+
+        size_t matrix_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * n_ * sizeof(double);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distances_, matrix_bytes), comm_);
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks((n_ + threads.x - 1) / threads.x,
+                          (n_ + threads.y - 1) / threads.y);
+        buildDistanceMatrix<<<blocks, threads>>>(d_points_, d_distances_, n_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), comm_);
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), comm_);
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char));
+        const size_t affordable = bytes_per_seed == 0 ? 1 : (free_bytes * 3 / 4) / bytes_per_seed;
+        batch_capacity_ = std::max(1, std::min(maximum_local_seeds,
+                                               static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(affordable, INT_MAX))));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_states_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_ * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
+        host_cardinalities_.resize(batch_capacity_);
     }

+    ~GpuWorkspace() {
+        cudaFree(d_cardinalities_); cudaFree(d_members_); cudaFree(d_states_);
+        cudaFree(d_seeds_); cudaFree(d_distances_); cudaFree(d_clustered_); cudaFree(d_points_);

+    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,
+                            const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered, double threshold,
+                            int& best_cardinality, int& best_seed,
+                            std::vector<int>& best_members) {

+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+        best_cardinality = -1;
+        best_seed = INT_MAX;
+        best_members.clear();
+        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {
+            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(batch_capacity_, seeds.size() - begin));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, seeds.data() + begin, static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
+            constructCandidateClusters<<<count, CUDA_THREADS>>>(

+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_cardinalities_.data(), d_cardinalities_,
+                                  static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
+            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = seeds[begin + i];
+                const int cardinality = host_cardinalities_[i];

+                    (cardinality == best_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {

+                    best_seed = seed;
+                    best_members.resize(cardinality);
+                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(),

-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {

-            }

-            // No more clusters can be formed

+
+private:
+    MPI_Comm comm_;
+    int n_, batch_capacity_;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    double* d_distances_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_states_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<int> host_cardinalities_;
+};

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
+                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
+
+    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
+    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+
+    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
+        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
+            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
+
+        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
+        std::vector<int> local_members;
+        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
+                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
+
+        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
+        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
+
+        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
+        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
+        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
+        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
+        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
+        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
+            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
+
+        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
+        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
+        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
+        #pragma omp parallel
+        {
+            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
+            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
+            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
+            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
+            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
+                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
         }
+        unclustered.clear();
+        for (auto& chunk : kept)
+            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

    61	
    62	inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    63	    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
    64	    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
    65	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    66	}
    67	
    68	__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
    69	                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
    70	    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    71	    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    72	    if (candidate < n && member < n) {
    73	        const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
    74	        const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
    75	        // Member-major storage makes all candidate loads in a warp contiguous.
    76	        distances[static_cast<size_t>(member) * n + candidate] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    77	    }
    78	}
    79	
    80	__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
    81	                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
    82	    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
    83	}
    84	
    85	// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
    86	// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
    87	__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
    88	                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
    89	                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
    90	                                           double threshold, int n,
    91	                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
    92	                                           int* __restrict__ members,
    93	                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
    94	    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
    95	    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
    96	
    97	    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
    98	    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
    99	    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
   100	    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
   101	    __syncthreads();
   102	
   103	    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
   104	    if (tid == 0) {
   105	        state[seed] = 1;
   106	        cluster_members[0] = seed;
   107	    }
   108	    __syncthreads();
   109	
   110	    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
   111	    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
   112	    __shared__ int member_count;
   113	    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
   114	    __syncthreads();
   115	
   116	    while (member_count < n) {
   117	        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
   118	        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
   119	        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
   120	            if (state[candidate]) continue;
   121	            double diameter = 0.0;
   122	            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
   123	                diameter = fmax(diameter,
   124	                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
   125	            }
   126	            if (diameter < threshold &&
   127	                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
   128	                thread_best_distance = diameter;
   129	                thread_best_index = candidate;
   130	            }
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
   134	        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
   135	        __syncthreads();
   136	        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
   137	            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
   138	                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
   139	                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
   140	                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
   141	                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
   142	            }
   143	            __syncthreads();
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
   147	        if (tid == 0) {
   148	            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
   149	            state[selected] = 1;
   150	            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
   151	        }
   152	        __syncthreads();
   153	    }
   154	    if (tid == 0) cardinalities[cluster_index] = member_count;
   155	}
   156	
   157	class GpuWorkspace {
   158	public:
   159	    GpuWorkspace(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds, MPI_Comm comm)
   160	        : comm_(comm), n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
   161	        int world_rank = 0;
   162	        MPI_Comm_rank(comm_, &world_rank);
   163	        MPI_Comm local_comm;
   164	        MPI_Comm_split_type(comm_, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, world_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);
   165	        int local_rank = 0, device_count = 0;
   166	        MPI_Comm_rank(local_comm, &local_rank);
   167	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count), comm_);
   168	        if (device_count == 0) {
   169	            if (world_rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA-capable GPU required.\n");
   170	            MPI_Abort(comm_, EXIT_FAILURE);
   171	        }
   172	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(local_rank % device_count), comm_);
   173	        MPI_Comm_free(&local_comm);
   174	
   175	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point)), comm_);
   176	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, static_cast<size_t>(n_)), comm_);
   177	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(Point),
   178	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
   179	
   180	        size_t matrix_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * n_ * sizeof(double);
   181	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distances_, matrix_bytes), comm_);
   182	        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
   183	        const dim3 blocks((n_ + threads.x - 1) / threads.x,
   184	                          (n_ + threads.y - 1) / threads.y);
   185	        buildDistanceMatrix<<<blocks, threads>>>(d_points_, d_distances_, n_);
   186	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
   187	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), comm_);
   188	
   189	        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
   190	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), comm_);
   191	        const size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char));
   192	        const size_t affordable = bytes_per_seed == 0 ? 1 : (free_bytes * 3 / 4) / bytes_per_seed;
   193	        batch_capacity_ = std::max(1, std::min(maximum_local_seeds,
   194	                                               static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(affordable, INT_MAX))));
   195	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
   196	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_states_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_), comm_);
   197	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * n_ * sizeof(int)), comm_);
   198	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, static_cast<size_t>(batch_capacity_) * sizeof(int)), comm_);
   199	        host_cardinalities_.resize(batch_capacity_);
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    ~GpuWorkspace() {
   203	        cudaFree(d_cardinalities_); cudaFree(d_members_); cudaFree(d_states_);
   204	        cudaFree(d_seeds_); cudaFree(d_distances_); cudaFree(d_clustered_); cudaFree(d_points_);
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    void bestLocalCandidate(const std::vector<int>& seeds,
   208	                            const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered, double threshold,
   209	                            int& best_cardinality, int& best_seed,
   210	                            std::vector<int>& best_members) {
   211	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(), static_cast<size_t>(n_),
   212	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
   213	        best_cardinality = -1;
   214	        best_seed = INT_MAX;
   215	        best_members.clear();
   216	        for (size_t begin = 0; begin < seeds.size(); begin += batch_capacity_) {
   217	            const int count = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(batch_capacity_, seeds.size() - begin));
   218	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, seeds.data() + begin, static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int),
   219	                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), comm_);
   220	            constructCandidateClusters<<<count, CUDA_THREADS>>>(
   221	                d_seeds_, count, d_clustered_, d_distances_, threshold, n_,
   222	                d_states_, d_members_, d_cardinalities_);
   223	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError(), comm_);
   224	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host_cardinalities_.data(), d_cardinalities_,
   225	                                  static_cast<size_t>(count) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), comm_);
   226	            for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   227	                const int seed = seeds[begin + i];
   228	                const int cardinality = host_cardinalities_[i];
   229	                if (cardinality > best_cardinality ||
   230	                    (cardinality == best_cardinality && seed < best_seed)) {
   250	    unsigned char* d_states_ = nullptr;
   251	    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
   252	    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
   253	    std::vector<int> host_cardinalities_;
   254	};
   255	
   256	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold,
   257	                                  MPI_Comm comm) {
   258	    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
   259	    MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
   260	    MPI_Comm_size(comm, &ranks);
   261	    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   262	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
   263	    std::vector<int> unclustered(n);
   264	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   265	    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) unclustered[i] = i;
   266	
   267	    const int maximum_local_seeds = (n + ranks - 1) / ranks;
   268	    GpuWorkspace gpu(points, std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds), comm);
   269	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   270	
   271	    while (!unclustered.empty()) {
   272	        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
   273	        local_seeds.reserve((unclustered.size() + ranks - 1) / ranks);
   274	        for (size_t i = rank; i < unclustered.size(); i += ranks)
   275	            local_seeds.push_back(unclustered[i]);
   276	
   277	        int local_cardinality, local_seed;
   278	        std::vector<int> local_members;
   279	        gpu.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold,
   280	                               local_cardinality, local_seed, local_members);
   281	
   282	        struct { int cardinality; int seed; } local_pair{local_cardinality, local_seed}, global_pair{};
   283	        MPI_Allreduce(&local_pair, &global_pair, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm);
   284	        if (global_pair.cardinality <= 0 || global_pair.seed == INT_MAX) break;
   285	
   286	        int owner = (local_seed == global_pair.seed) ? rank : -1;
   287	        MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &owner, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, comm);
   288	        std::vector<int> selected_members(global_pair.cardinality);
   289	        if (rank == owner) selected_members = local_members;
   290	        MPI_Bcast(selected_members.data(), global_pair.cardinality, MPI_INT, owner, comm);
   291	        clusters.push_back({selected_members, global_pair.seed});
   292	
   293	        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   294	        for (int i = 0; i < global_pair.cardinality; ++i)
   295	            clustered[selected_members[i]] = 1;
   296	
   297	        // Static chunks plus ordered concatenation preserve the ascending seed order.
   298	        const int thread_count = omp_get_max_threads();
   299	        std::vector<std::vector<int>> kept(thread_count);
   300	        #pragma omp parallel
   301	        {
   302	            const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();
   303	            const size_t begin = unclustered.size() * tid / omp_get_num_threads();
   304	            const size_t end = unclustered.size() * (tid + 1) / omp_get_num_threads();
   305	            kept[tid].reserve(end - begin);
   306	            for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i)
   307	                if (!clustered[unclustered[i]]) kept[tid].push_back(unclustered[i]);
   308	        }
   309	        unclustered.clear();
   310	        for (auto& chunk : kept)
   311	            unclustered.insert(unclustered.end(), chunk.begin(), chunk.end());
   312	    }
   313	    return clusters;
   314	}
   315	
   316	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   317	                      const std::vector<Point>& points, double threshold) {
   318	    bool valid = true;
   319	    std::printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   320	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   321	        const Cluster& cluster = clusters[c];
   322	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   323	        #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:max_diameter) schedule(dynamic)
   324	        for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(cluster.members.size()); ++i) {
   325	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j)
   326	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter,
   327	                    pointDistance(points[cluster.members[i]], points[cluster.members[j]]));
   328	        }
   329	        if (c < 10)
   330	            std::printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n",
   331	                        c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   332	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) {
   333	            std::printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n",
   334	                        c, max_diameter, threshold);
   335	            valid = false;
   336	        }
   337	    }
   338	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   339	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   340	        for (int member : clusters[c].members) {
   341	            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
   342	                std::printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
   343	                            member, membership[member], c);
   344	                valid = false;
   345	            }
   346	            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	    int clustered_count = 0;
   350	    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+:clustered_count)
   351	    for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(membership.size()); ++i)
   352	        clustered_count += membership[i] >= 0;
   353	    std::printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
   354	                points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
   355	    return valid;
   356	}
   357	
   358	void printUsage(const char* program) {
   359	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
   360	    std::printf("Options:\n  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
   361	    std::printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
   362	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   363	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   364	}
   365	
   366	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   367	    int provided = 0;
   368	    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
   369	    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
   370	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   371	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
   372	    int num_points = 1000;
   373	    double threshold = 2.0;
   374	    bool validate = false, print_results_requested = false;
   375	    int exit_code = 0;
   376	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   377	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) num_points = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   378	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) threshold = std::atof(argv[++i]);
   379	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) validate = true;
   380	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) print_results_requested = true;
   381	        else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) { if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]); MPI_Finalize(); return 0; }
   382	        else { if (rank == 0) { std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]); printUsage(argv[0]); }
   383	               MPI_Finalize(); return 1; }
   384	    }
   385	    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
   386	        if (rank == 0) std::printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n",
   387	                                   num_points, threshold);
   388	        MPI_Finalize(); return 1;
   389	    }
   390	    if (rank == 0) {
   391	        std::printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\nNumber of points: %d\nDistance threshold: %.2f\n",
   392	                    num_points, threshold);
   393	        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n",
   394	                    validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
   395	    }
   396	
   397	    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
   398	    if (rank == 0) generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
   399	    MPI_Bcast(points.data(), num_points * static_cast<int>(sizeof(Point)), MPI_BYTE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   400	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   401	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   402	    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   403	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   404	    const double local_elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   405	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   406	    MPI_Reduce(&local_elapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   407	
   408	    if (rank == 0) {
   409	        const long elapsed_ms = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   410	        std::printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\nClusters found: %zu\n", elapsed_ms, clusters.size());
   411	        int total_clustered = 0, max_cluster_size = 0;
   412	        #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+:total_clustered) reduction(max:max_cluster_size)
   413	        for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(clusters.size()); ++i) {
   414	            const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
   415	            total_clustered += size;
   416	            max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
   417	        }
   418	        const double average = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :
   419	            static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
   420	        std::printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\nAverage cluster size: %.2f\nMaximum cluster size: %d\n",
   421	                    total_clustered, num_points, 100.0 * total_clustered / num_points,
   422	                    average, max_cluster_size);
   423	        const double safe_elapsed = std::max(elapsed, std::numeric_limits<double>::min());
   424	        std::printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n",
   425	                    clusters.size() / safe_elapsed, num_points / safe_elapsed);
   426	
   427	        if (print_results_requested) {
   428	            std::vector<double> membership_data(num_points, -1.0);
   429	            for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c)
   430	                for (int member : clusters[c].members) membership_data[member] = static_cast<double>(c);
   431	            print_results(membership_data, "ClusterMembership");
   432	        }
   433	        if (validate) {
   434	            const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
   435	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   436	            exit_code = valid ? 0 : 1;
   437	        }
   438	    }
   439	    MPI_Bcast(&exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   440	    MPI_Finalize();
   441	    return exit_code;
   442	}

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                    double* __restrict__ distances, int n) {
+    const int candidate = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const int member = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double distance_a, int index_a,
+                                                 double distance_b, int index_b) {
+    return distance_a < distance_b || (distance_a == distance_b && index_a < index_b);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Candidate points are evaluated in
+// parallel; the ordered (diameter,index) reduction exactly implements the serial tie rule.
+__global__ void constructCandidateClusters(const int* __restrict__ seeds, int seed_count,
+                                           const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                           const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                           double threshold, int n,
+                                           unsigned char* __restrict__ states,
+                                           int* __restrict__ members,
+                                           int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int cluster_index = blockIdx.x;
+    if (cluster_index >= seed_count) return;
+
+    const int tid = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned char* state = states + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    int* cluster_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(cluster_index) * n;
+    for (int p = tid; p < n; p += blockDim.x) state[p] = clustered[p];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const int seed = seeds[cluster_index];
+    if (tid == 0) {
+        state[seed] = 1;
+        cluster_members[0] = seed;
     }

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduce_distance[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int reduce_index[CUDA_THREADS];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    if (tid == 0) member_count = 1;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (member_count < n) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_index = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = tid; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (state[candidate]) continue;
+            double diameter = 0.0;
+            for (int m = 0; m < member_count; ++m) {
+                diameter = fmax(diameter,
+                    distances[static_cast<size_t>(cluster_members[m]) * n + candidate]);
+            }
+            if (diameter < threshold &&
+                betterCandidate(diameter, candidate, thread_best_distance, thread_best_index)) {
+                thread_best_distance = diameter;
+                thread_best_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduce_distance[tid] = thread_best_distance;
+        reduce_index[tid] = thread_best_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (tid < offset && betterCandidate(reduce_distance[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_index[tid + offset],
+                                                reduce_distance[tid], reduce_index[tid])) {
+                reduce_distance[tid] = reduce_distance[tid + offset];
+                reduce_index[tid] = reduce_index[tid + offset];
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (reduce_index[0] == INT_MAX) break;
+        if (tid == 0) {
+            const int selected = reduce_index[0];
+            state[selected] = 1;
+            cluster_members[member_count++] = selected;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-

+    if (tid == 0) cardinalities[cluster_index] = member_count;
 }
 
-// Main QT clustering algorithm
-std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
-                                  const double threshold) {
-    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
